Add a payslip from a document

Photo · Screenshot · PDF

Choose a payslip photo, screenshot, or PDF. ShiftTrack will try to read it and fill a draft. Review every value before saving. Processing happens in this browser; the document is not uploaded to ShiftTrack.

Supported: JPG, PNG, WebP and PDF. For best results use a clear, straight image.
OCR can misread dates, decimal points and amounts. Verify every field. Nothing is saved until you choose “Use extracted values” and then “Save payslip record”.
Each amount is matched to the payslip gross to calculate its effective rate. Remove items that are not deductions.
